[  10.306][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 73] float bt1886_eotf(float V, float gamma, float Lw, float Lb) {
[  10.306][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 74]     float a = pow(pow(Lw, 1.0 / gamma) - pow(Lb, 1.0 / gamma), gamma);
[  10.306][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 75]     float b = pow(Lb, 1.0 / gamma) / (pow(Lw, 1.0 / gamma) - pow(Lb, 1.0 / gamma));
[  10.306][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 76]     float L = a * pow(max(V + b, 0.0), gamma);
[  10.306][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 77]     return L;
[  10.306][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 78] }

[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 22] 
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 23] // Hue-dependent natural-colour protection derived from the 140-patch X-Rite
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 24] // ColorChecker Digital SG reference data released in 2016 for charts made
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 25] // after November 2014 (D50 Lab -> Bradford D65 -> Oklab).
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 26] //
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 27] // The table holds an absolute Oklab chroma, computed without reference to any
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 28] // gamut, so the profile is a property of the reference colours alone and does
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 29] // not move with the source or destination primaries. Near-neutral samples
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 30] // (C < 0.02) have unstable hue and are excluded; for the remaining 81 patches
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 31] // the median chroma plus 2.5% forms the floor, and every more saturated patch
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 32] // adds a 12-degree Gaussian lobe, the lobes combining through an eighth-order
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 33] // smooth norm. The pixel stage clamps the result against the output boundary,
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 34] // which is what tapers the protection towards black and white; neutral samples
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 35] // are protected separately by the radial construction and remain unchanged.
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 36] // The periodic table is sampled cubically so sparse chart hues do not become
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 37] // polygonal boundary corners.
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 38] // Reference data: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.3245895

[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 60] float monotone_slope(float previous_delta, float next_delta) {
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 61]     if (previous_delta * next_delta <= 0.0)
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 62]         return 0.0;
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 63] 
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 64]     float magnitude = min(
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 65]         min(abs(previous_delta), abs(next_delta)),
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 66]         0.5 * abs(previous_delta + next_delta)
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 67]     );
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 68]     return sign(previous_delta) * magnitude;
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 69] }
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 70] 
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 71] float monotone_cubic(
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 72]     float p0,
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 73]     float p1,
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 74]     float p2,
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 75]     float p3,
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 76]     float weight
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 77] ) {
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 78]     float m1 = monotone_slope(p1 - p0, p2 - p1);
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 79]     float m2 = monotone_slope(p2 - p1, p3 - p2);
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 80]     float weight2 = weight * weight;
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 81]     float weight3 = weight2 * weight;
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 82]     return (2.0 * weight3 - 3.0 * weight2 + 1.0) * p1
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 83]          + (weight3 - 2.0 * weight2 + weight) * m1
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 84]          + (-2.0 * weight3 + 3.0 * weight2) * p2
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 85]          + (weight3 - weight2) * m2;
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 86] }
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 87] 
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 88] float sample_colorchecker_protection(float hue) {
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 89]     float position = fract(hue * INV_TAU + 1.0) *
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 90]                      float(COLORCHECKER_PROTECTION_SIZE);
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 91]     int index = int(floor(position));
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 92]     float weight = fract(position);
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 93]     int mask = COLORCHECKER_PROTECTION_SIZE - 1;
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 94]     float p0 = COLORCHECKER_PROTECTION[(index - 1) & mask];
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 95]     float p1 = COLORCHECKER_PROTECTION[index & mask];
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 96]     float p2 = COLORCHECKER_PROTECTION[(index + 1) & mask];
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 97]     float p3 = COLORCHECKER_PROTECTION[(index + 2) & mask];
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 98]     return monotone_cubic(p0, p1, p2, p3, weight);
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 99] }
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [100] 
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [101] const mat3 OKLAB_TO_LMS = mat3(
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [102]     1.0000000000000000,  0.3963377773761749,  0.2158037573099136,
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [103]     1.0000000000000000, -0.1055613458156586, -0.0638541728258133,
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [104]     1.0000000000000000, -0.0894841775298119, -1.2914855480194092
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [105] );
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [106] 
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [107] const mat3 LMS_TO_XYZ = mat3(
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [108]      1.2268798758459243, -0.5578149944602171,  0.2813910456659647,
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [109]     -0.0405757452148008,  1.1122868032803170, -0.0717110580655164,
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [110]     -0.0763729366746601, -0.4214933324022432,  1.5869240198367816
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [111] );
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [112] 
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [113] const mat3 XYZ_TO_BT2020 = mat3(
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [114]      1.716651187971268, -0.355670783776392, -0.253366281373660,
[  10.354][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [115]     -0.666684351832489,  1.616481236634939,  0.0157685458139111,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [116]      0.017639857445311, -0.042770613257809,  0.942103121235474
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [117] );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [118] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [119] const mat3 XYZ_TO_DISPLAY_P3 = mat3(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [120]      2.493496911941425,  -0.931383617919124,  -0.402710784450717,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [121]     -0.829488969561575,   1.762664060318346,   0.023624685841944,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [122]      0.035845830243784,  -0.076172389268041,   0.956884524007687
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [123] );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [124] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [125] vec3 oklch_to_xyz(float lightness, float chroma, vec2 hue_direction) {
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [126]     vec3 lab = vec3(lightness, chroma * hue_direction);
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [127]     vec3 lms_root = lab * OKLAB_TO_LMS;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [128]     vec3 lms = lms_root * lms_root * lms_root;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [129]     return lms * LMS_TO_XYZ;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [130] }
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [131] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [132] bool rgb_in_unit_gamut(vec3 rgb) {
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [133]     return all(greaterThanEqual(rgb, vec3(0.0))) &&
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [134]            all(lessThanEqual(rgb, vec3(1.0)));
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [135] }
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [136] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [137] float find_chroma_boundary(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [138]     float lightness,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [139]     vec2 hue_direction,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [140]     mat3 xyz_to_rgb
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [141] ) {
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [142]     float lower = 0.0;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [143]     float upper = 1.0;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [144] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [145]     for (int i = 0; i < BOUNDARY_SEARCH_ITERATIONS; i++) {
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [146]         float candidate = 0.5 * (lower + upper);
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [147]         vec3 xyz = oklch_to_xyz(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [148]             lightness,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [149]             candidate,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [150]             hue_direction
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [151]         );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [152]         vec3 rgb = xyz * xyz_to_rgb;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [153] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [154]         if (rgb_in_unit_gamut(rgb))
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [155]             lower = candidate;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [156]         else
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [157]             upper = candidate;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [158]     }
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [159] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [160]     return lower;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [161] }
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [162] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [163] void generate_gamut_boundary(ivec2 position) {
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [164]     if (position.x >= int(BOUNDARY_HUE_SIZE) ||
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [165]         position.y >= int(BOUNDARY_LIGHTNESS_SIZE)) {
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [166]         return;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [167]     }
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [168] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [169]     float hue = TAU * float(position.x) / float(BOUNDARY_HUE_SIZE);
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [170]     float lightness = float(position.y) /
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [171]                       float(BOUNDARY_LIGHTNESS_SIZE - 1u);
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [172]     vec2 hue_direction = vec2(cos(hue), sin(hue));
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [173]     float input_chroma = find_chroma_boundary(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [174]         lightness,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [175]         hue_direction,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [176]         XYZ_TO_BT2020
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [177]     );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [178]     float output_chroma = find_chroma_boundary(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [179]         lightness,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [180]         hue_direction,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [181]         XYZ_TO_DISPLAY_P3
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [182]     );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [183]     float protection_chroma = sample_colorchecker_protection(hue);
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [184] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [185]     imageStore(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [186]         out_image,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [187]         position,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [188]         vec4(input_chroma, output_chroma, protection_chroma, 1.0)
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [189]     );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [190] }
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [191] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [192] void hook() {
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [193]     generate_gamut_boundary(ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID.xy));
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [194] }
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [195] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [196] //!HOOK OUTPUT
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [197] //!BIND HOOKED
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [198] //!BIND GAMUT_BOUNDARY
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [199] //!DESC gamut mapping (bottosson)
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [200] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [201] // GAMUT_BOUNDARY stores the input and output chroma and the ColorChecker-derived
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [202] // protection chroma. Clamping the interpolated protection against the
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [203] // interpolated output boundary is what keeps a protected colour inside the
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [204] // output gamut. Their remaining shell provides room to turn gradually towards
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [205] // the smooth guide gamut before RGB fitting.
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [206] const float CHROMA_EPSILON = 1e-6;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [207] // Avoid an arbitrarily steep shell where both gamut boundaries converge at
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [208] // black and white. Those nearly neutral endpoints need no full guide pull.
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [209] const float SHELL_MIN_CHROMA_SPAN = 0.01;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [210] // The protection profile is an absolute chroma, so it needs the output
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [211] // boundary to bound it. The cap reserves the outermost 2% for the shell and
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [212] // the blend keeps the hand-over differentiable where the cap takes over.
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [213] const float PROTECTION_CAP = 0.98;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [214] const float PROTECTION_CAP_BLEND = 0.01;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [215] const float GUIDE_ANCHOR_LIGHTNESS = 0.70;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [216] const float GUIDE_KNEE_SCALE = 0.85;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [217] const float SOFT_CLIP_GREY = 0.20;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [218] const float TAU = 6.28318530717958647692;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [219] const float INV_TAU = 1.0 / TAU;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [220] const int BOUNDARY_HUE_SIZE = 256;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [221] const int BOUNDARY_LIGHTNESS_SIZE = 128;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [222] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [223] const mat3 BT2020_TO_XYZ = mat3(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [224]     0.6369580483012914, 0.1446169035862083, 0.1688809751641721,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [225]     0.2627002120112671, 0.6779980715188708, 0.0593017164698620,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [226]     0.0000000000000000, 0.0280726930490874, 1.0609850577107910
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [227] );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [228] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [229] const mat3 XYZ_TO_DISPLAY_P3 = mat3(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [230]      2.493496911941425,  -0.931383617919124,  -0.402710784450717,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [231]     -0.829488969561575,   1.762664060318346,   0.023624685841944,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [232]      0.035845830243784,  -0.076172389268041,   0.956884524007687
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [233] );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [234] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [235] const mat3 XYZ_TO_LMS = mat3(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [236]     0.8190224379967030, 0.3619062600528904, -0.1288737815209879,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [237]     0.0329836539323885, 0.9292868615863434,  0.0361446663506424,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [238]     0.0481771893596242, 0.2642395317527308,  0.6335478284694309
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [239] );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [240] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [241] const mat3 LMS_TO_XYZ = mat3(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [242]      1.2268798758459243, -0.5578149944602171,  0.2813910456659647,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [243]     -0.0405757452148008,  1.1122868032803170, -0.0717110580655164,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [244]     -0.0763729366746601, -0.4214933324022432,  1.5869240198367816
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [245] );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [246] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [247] const mat3 LMS_TO_OKLAB = mat3(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [248]     0.2104542683093140,  0.7936177747023054, -0.0040720430116193,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [249]     1.9779985324311684, -2.4285922420485799,  0.4505937096174110,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [250]     0.0259040424655478,  0.7827717124575296, -0.8086757549230774
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [251] );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [252] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [253] const mat3 OKLAB_TO_LMS = mat3(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [254]     1.0000000000000000,  0.3963377773761749,  0.2158037573099136,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [255]     1.0000000000000000, -0.1055613458156586, -0.0638541728258133,
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [256]     1.0000000000000000, -0.0894841775298119, -1.2914855480194092
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [257] );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [258] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [259] float signed_cuberoot(float value) {
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [260]     return sign(value) * pow(abs(value), 1.0 / 3.0);
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [261] }
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [262] 
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [263] vec3 xyz_to_oklab(vec3 xyz) {
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [264]     vec3 lms = xyz * XYZ_TO_LMS;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [265]     vec3 lms_root = vec3(
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [266]         signed_cuberoot(lms.x),
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [267]         signed_cuberoot(lms.y),
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [268]         signed_cuberoot(lms.z)
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [269]     );
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [270]     return lms_root * LMS_TO_OKLAB;
[  10.355][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [271] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [272] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [273] vec3 oklab_to_xyz(vec3 lab) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [274]     vec3 lms_root = lab * OKLAB_TO_LMS;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [275]     vec3 lms = lms_root * lms_root * lms_root;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [276]     return lms * LMS_TO_XYZ;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [277] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [278] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [279] vec3 oklab_to_oklch(vec3 lab) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [280]     float chroma = length(lab.yz);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [281]     float hue = chroma > CHROMA_EPSILON ? atan(lab.z, lab.y) : 0.0;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [282]     return vec3(lab.x, chroma, hue);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [283] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [284] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [285] float normalized_hue(float hue) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [286]     return fract(hue * INV_TAU + 1.0);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [287] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [288] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [289] vec3 fetch_boundary_texel(int hue_index, int lightness_index) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [290]     vec3 value = texelFetch(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [291]         GAMUT_BOUNDARY_raw,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [292]         ivec2(hue_index, lightness_index),
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [293]         0
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [294]     ).rgb;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [295]     return GAMUT_BOUNDARY_mul * value;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [296] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [297] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [298] // GAMUT_BOUNDARY already carries the generator's hue-periodic filtering, so
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [299] // four texels are enough: the cusps where the boundary turns sharply are
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [300] // narrower than the table's hue spacing and stay unresolved by any stencil.
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [301] vec3 sample_gamut_boundary(float lightness, float hue) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [302]     float hue_position = normalized_hue(hue) *
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [303]                          float(BOUNDARY_HUE_SIZE);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [304]     int hue_lower = int(floor(hue_position)) % BOUNDARY_HUE_SIZE;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [305]     int hue_upper = (hue_lower + 1) % BOUNDARY_HUE_SIZE;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [306]     float hue_weight = fract(hue_position);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [307] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [308]     float lightness_position = clamp(lightness, 0.0, 1.0) *
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [309]                                float(BOUNDARY_LIGHTNESS_SIZE - 1);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [310]     int lightness_lower = int(floor(lightness_position));
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [311]     int lightness_upper = min(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [312]         lightness_lower + 1,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [313]         BOUNDARY_LIGHTNESS_SIZE - 1
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [314]     );
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [315]     float lightness_weight = fract(lightness_position);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [316] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [317]     vec3 lower = mix(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [318]         fetch_boundary_texel(hue_lower, lightness_lower),
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [319]         fetch_boundary_texel(hue_upper, lightness_lower),
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [320]         hue_weight
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [321]     );
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [322]     vec3 upper = mix(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [323]         fetch_boundary_texel(hue_lower, lightness_upper),
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [324]         fetch_boundary_texel(hue_upper, lightness_upper),
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [325]         hue_weight
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [326]     );
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [327]     return mix(lower, upper, lightness_weight);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [328] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [329] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [330] float smooth_shell_weight(float position) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [331]     return position * position * (3.0 - 2.0 * position);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [332] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [333] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [334] // Polynomial smooth minimum: the exact minimum outside the blend width and
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [335] // differentiable across it. A plain minimum would leave a crease in the
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [336] // protection wherever the profile hands over to the output cap.
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [337] float smooth_minimum(float a, float b, float blend) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [338]     float weight = clamp(0.5 + 0.5 * (b - a) / blend, 0.0, 1.0);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [339]     return mix(b, a, weight) - blend * weight * (1.0 - weight);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [340] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [341] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [342] // A rotationally symmetric, rounded chroma guide inspired by Bjorn Ottosson's
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [343] // Extended Oklab and two-stage gamut-reduction experiment. It provides a
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [344] // smooth target without importing the RGB cube's hue-dependent cusp.
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [345] float guide_gamut_radius(vec2 direction) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [346]     return max(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [347]         0.5 - 0.2 * direction.x
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [348]             + 0.2 * direction.x * direction.y
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [349]             - 0.15 * direction.y,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [350]         CHROMA_EPSILON
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [351]     );
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [352] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [353] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [354] float compress_guide_radius(float radius, float limit) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [355]     float knee = GUIDE_KNEE_SCALE * limit;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [356]     if (radius <= knee)
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [357]         return radius;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [358] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [359]     float span = max(limit - knee, CHROMA_EPSILON);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [360]     float excess = (radius - knee) / span;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [361]     return knee + span * excess / (1.0 + excess);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [362] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [363] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [364] vec3 reduce_to_guide_gamut(vec3 lab, float weight) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [365]     float chroma = length(lab.yz);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [366]     vec2 radial = vec2(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [367]         lab.x - GUIDE_ANCHOR_LIGHTNESS,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [368]         chroma
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [369]     );
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [370]     float radius = length(radial);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [371] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [372]     if (radius <= CHROMA_EPSILON)
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [373]         return lab;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [374] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [375]     vec2 direction = radial / radius;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [376]     float limit = guide_gamut_radius(direction);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [377]     float mapped_radius = compress_guide_radius(radius, limit);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [378]     float mapped_chroma = direction.y * mapped_radius;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [379]     float chroma_scale = mapped_chroma /
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [380]                          max(chroma, CHROMA_EPSILON);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [381] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [382]     // Moving the radial lightness component towards the 0.7 guide anchor can
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [383]     // make a more saturated highlight darker than its surround. Keep Oklab L
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [384]     // fixed and use the guide only to derive a smooth chroma target; the
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [385]     // outside-only RGB stage handles the remaining cube fit.
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [386]     vec3 target = vec3(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [387]         lab.x,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [388]         lab.yz * chroma_scale
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [389]     );
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [390]     return mix(lab, target, weight);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [391] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [392] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [393] vec3 map_to_guide_gamut(vec3 lab) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [394]     vec3 lch = oklab_to_oklch(lab);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [395]     float lightness = clamp(lch.x, 0.0, 1.0);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [396]     vec3 boundary = sample_gamut_boundary(lightness, lch.z);
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [397]     float protection_boundary = smooth_minimum(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [398]         boundary.z,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [399]         PROTECTION_CAP * boundary.y,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [400]         PROTECTION_CAP_BLEND
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [401]     );
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [402] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [403]     if (lch.y <= protection_boundary)
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [404]         return lab;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [405] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [406]     float input_span = max(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [407]         boundary.x - protection_boundary,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [408]         SHELL_MIN_CHROMA_SPAN
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [409]     );
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [410]     float input_position = clamp(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [411]         (lch.y - protection_boundary) / input_span,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [412]         0.0,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [413]         1.0
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [414]     );
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [415]     return reduce_to_guide_gamut(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [416]         lab,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [417]         smooth_shell_weight(input_position)
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [418]     );
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [419] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [420] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [421] vec3 soft_saturate(vec3 value, float softness) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [422]     float a = 1.0 + softness;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [423]     value = min(value, vec3(a));
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [424]     float b = (a - 1.0) * sqrt(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [425]         a / max(2.0 - a, CHROMA_EPSILON)
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [426]     );
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [427]     float denominator = max(
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [428]         sqrt(a * a + b * b) - b,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [429]         CHROMA_EPSILON
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [430]     );
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [431]     return vec3(1.0) -
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [432]            (sqrt((value - a) * (value - a) + b * b) - b) /
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [433]            denominator;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [434] }
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [435] 
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [436] // Identity for RGB values already inside [0, 1]. When any channel is outside,
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [437] // all channels share one softness so edges and corners are approached without
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [438] // a hue-preserving snap.
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [439] // A fourth-order norm blends simultaneous channel excursions smoothly; a
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [440] // hard maximum leaves a faint crease where the limiting RGB channel changes.
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [441] // Based on "soft clipping scaled to only have an effect if the color is
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [442] // outside the gamut":
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [443] vec3 soft_clip_rgb_outside_only(vec3 color) {
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [444]     // Exact: the reparameterization below maps the unit cube onto itself.
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [445]     if (all(greaterThanEqual(color, vec3(0.0))) &&
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [446]         all(lessThanEqual(color, vec3(1.0))))
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [447]         return color;
[  10.356][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [448] 
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [449]     vec3 centered = color - SOFT_CLIP_GREY;
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [450]     vec3 direction = sign(centered);
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [451]     vec3 scale = 0.5 + direction * (0.5 - SOFT_CLIP_GREY);
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [452]     vec3 magnitude = abs(centered / scale);
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [453]     vec3 outside = max(magnitude - vec3(1.0), vec3(0.0));
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [454]     vec3 outside2 = outside * outside;
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [455]     float outside_norm = pow(dot(outside2, outside2), 0.25);
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [456]     float softness = outside_norm / (1.0 + outside_norm);
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [457]     vec3 mapped = soft_saturate(magnitude, softness);
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [458]     return SOFT_CLIP_GREY + scale * direction * mapped;
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [459] }
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [460] 
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [461] vec4 hook() {
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [462]     vec4 color = HOOKED_mul * textureLod(HOOKED_raw, HOOKED_pos, 0.0);
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [463]     vec3 xyz = color.rgb * BT2020_TO_XYZ;
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [464]     vec3 lab = xyz_to_oklab(xyz);
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [465]     lab = map_to_guide_gamut(lab);
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [466]     vec3 mapped_xyz = oklab_to_xyz(lab);
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [467]     vec3 mapped_rgb = mapped_xyz * XYZ_TO_DISPLAY_P3;
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [468]     color.rgb = soft_clip_rgb_outside_only(mapped_rgb);
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [469]     color.rgb = clamp(color.rgb, 0.0, 1.0);
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [470]     return color;
[  10.357][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [471] }

[  10.370][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [  3]

[  10.370][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 10] 
[  10.370][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 11] float monitor_curve_eotf_inv(float y) {
[  10.370][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 12]     const float yb = pow(offset * gamma / ((gamma - 1.0) * (1.0 + offset)), gamma);
[  10.370][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 13]     const float rs = pow((gamma - 1.0) / offset, gamma - 1.0) * pow((1.0 + offset) / gamma, gamma);
[  10.370][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 14]     return y >= yb ? (1.0 + offset) * pow(y, 1.0 / gamma) - offset : y * rs;
[  10.370][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 15] }

[  10.370][d][vo/gpu-next/libplacebo] [ 22]     );
